SCANNED CONTACT DIRECTORY — REVIEW EDITION
Source: Jeffrey_Epstein_UNKNOWN.pdf
Coverage: all 95 PDF pages; page numbers below are PDF positions, not printed labels.
Structured records / headings: 1,614 (NOT a count of unique people).
Visually transcribed records: 200 (PDF pages 1–10 and 93–95).
Machine-transcribed records / headings: 1,414 (PDF pages 11–92).
Additional heading-only checks do not verify the associated contact details.

IMPORTANT ACCURACY LIMITS
This is a source-linked extraction and review draft, not a fully proofread contact database.
Machine text can contain wrong letters, digits, missing text and imperfect entry boundaries.
The scan is authoritative. Use the HTML companion to compare an entry with its source image.
Brackets identify uncertainty or explanatory transcription notes; they are not original wording.
No hidden/redacted content was reconstructed. No missing names, numbers or email endings were
completed from outside sources. The original PDF text layer is omitted for records touching
page 86 because the scan contains visible blackouts there.

SOURCE ORGANIZATION
Reading order is page, active column left-to-right, then top-to-bottom within each column.
Joint names and duplicate listings are retained, not split into invented individual records.
A contact listed within another entry (assistant, family, staff, driver, etc.) stays with its
original label. Search the complete contact text / Contact lines sheet for these nested names.
Continuation assignments outside the visually transcribed pages require source review.
Source type abbreviations and historical dialing formats are retained; numbers are text.
The phone/email/address buckets are aids only: the complete line-by-line transcription is
available in the workbook and the original images are available in the HTML companion.
There was no current-contact validation or outside research. The book and its handwritten
notes do not by themselves establish a person's conduct or involvement in wrongdoing.

FILES
Contact_directory_review.xlsx — filterable directory, line-by-line text, raw readings, page index.
Contact_directory_review.html — searchable offline directory with all 95 original page images.
Contact_directory_review.txt — entries in source reading order, with status and locations.
Contact_directory_data.json — structured data including bounding boxes and both readings.
Source_column_transcripts.txt — unsplit column OCR for reviewing missed/spurious entry boundaries.
